The hp namespace URI\n    hp_ns = 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\n    para_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}p'\n\n    paras = []\n    for para in element.iter(para_tag):\n        text_content = ''.join(para.itertext())\n        if '{{' in text_content:\n            paras.append(para)\n    return paras\n\n\ndef substitute_in_paragraph(para, replacements, nsmap):\n    \"\"\"Replace placeholders inside a paragraph and remove layout-cache children.\"\"\"\n    hp_ns = 'http://www.hancom.co.kr/hwpml/2010/HWPML'\n    t_tag = f'{{{hp_ns}}}t'\n\n    # Gather all <hp:t> descendants in order\n    t_elements = [child for child in para.iter(t_tag)]\n    if not t_elements:\n        return False\n\n    # Concatenate their .text values\n    combined = ''.join(t.text or '' for t in t_elements)\n    if '{{' not in combined:\n        return False\n\n    # Perform replacement on combined string\n    def replacer(match):\n        key = match.group(1)\n        return replacements.get(key, match.group(0))\n\n    new_combined = PLACEHOLDER_RE.sub(replacer, combined)\n\n    #
